- **bluefin-architecture**: System shape and design rationale for the Bluefin reference implementation. Covers the three-crate split, the dual-socket model (one listener for handshake + one connected socket per data connection), the connection-demux table, the buffer-with-waker cross-task synchronisation primitive, the per-connection task topology, and the threading/affinity model. Explains *why* the system looks the way it does and what invariants the layout depends on. Pair with `bluefin-protocol` for the wire format and with `bluefin-101` for "where does this live in the code". - **blueprint-fundamentals**: A **Blueprint** is a `UObject`-derived class authored as an asset. When compiled it produces a `UBlueprintGeneratedClass` — a fully first-class `UClass` that the engine treats identically to any C++ class at runtime. Understanding this two-object architecture (editor asset vs. runtime class) is the foundation for all Blueprint + C++ work. ([ziyarduc/Baran-bk-game](https://github.com/ziyarduc/Baran-bk-game/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/blueprint-fundamentals) · ★1)
- **blueprint-gate**: Run a pass/fail gate check against a filled Blueprint (blueprint/blueprint-template.md) before it moves from DEFINE to DELIVER — before any feature code, and again before ship. ([buildermethods/bm-skills](https://github.com/buildermethods/bm-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bm-prd-creator) · ★155 · 2 copies)
- **bm-public-api**: BaseMachinaの公開API（REST API）を外部system、CI/CD、自社scriptから呼び出すcodeを書くskill。環境・actionの一覧と詳細、action実行、review依頼の作成・状態取得・承認後実行を扱う。`bm login`のJWT、GitHub Actions・Google Cloud・AWS・自社OIDC IdPのID Token交換、response・error処理、OpenAPI client生成を実装するときに使う。action定義や`bm sync`はbm-code-management、仕様調査だけならbasemachina-docsを使う。実際のAPI呼び出しは行わない。 ([basemachina/skills](https://github.com/basemachina/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/bm-public-api) · ★2)
- **bm-remember**: Quickly capture a thought, fact, or reminder into Basic Memory as a lightweight note. Use when the user says "remember that…", "note this", "save this to memory", or runs /basic-memory:bm-remember. For quick deliberate capture — not full decision or session records. ([basicmachines-co/basic-memory](https://github.com/basicmachines-co/basic-memory/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude-code/skills/bm-remember) · ★4.6k · 9 copies)
- **bm-remember**: Quickly save a small fact, reminder, or user preference into Basic Memory from Codex without turning it into a full decision or checkpoint. ([basicmachines-co/basic-memory](https://github.com/basicmachines-co/basic-memory/tree/HEAD/plugins/codex/skills/bm-remember) · ★4.6k)
- **bm-scribe:add-note**: Use when creating a personal note on a topic. Creates `&lt;USERNAME&gt;/notes/&lt;slug&gt;/main` via the three-step pattern. ([patiently/anti-tangent-mcp](https://github.com/patiently/anti-tangent-mcp/tree/HEAD/plugin/bm-scribe/skills/add-note) · ★1)
- **bm-scribe:add-todo**: Use when adding a personal todo to your rolling list. Appends a `- [ ] &lt;text&gt;` bullet to `&lt;USERNAME&gt;/todo/main`. Creates the note on first use. ([patiently/anti-tangent-mcp](https://github.com/patiently/anti-tangent-mcp/tree/HEAD/plugin/bm-scribe/skills/add-todo) · ★1)
- **bm-scribe:create-decision**: Use when creating a new project-knowledge decision note. Walks through the three-step BM v0.21.1 pattern and lands the note at the canonical v0.7.0 permalink `&lt;PROJECT&gt;/decisions/&lt;NNNN&gt;-&lt;slug&gt;/main`. ([patiently/anti-tangent-mcp](https://github.com/patiently/anti-tangent-mcp/tree/HEAD/plugin/bm-scribe/skills/create-decision) · ★1)
